- **fm-review**: Code review of a Font Fu story branch (Rust workspace + Tauri 2 / Vue 3). Full mode: the parent captures the diff once, picks the lenses the diff needs (SPEC, COR, TST always; REUSE, FFI, A11Y when the diff calls for them), runs them as isolated parallel subagents, merges duplicates, then runs ONE adversarial verifier over the blocking findings so only reproduced defects block. Verify mode (review cycles 2+): no new lens fan-out — one subagent checks whether the prior blocking findings are fixed and whether the fix delta introduced a new blocking defect. Writes one file under .harness/reviews… ([goranocokoljic/font-manager](https://github.com/goranocokoljic/font-manager/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/fm-review.md))
